# Cloudflare Partner Locator

> Source: https://partnerlocator.cloudflare.com/dashboard/

# Cloudflare Partner Locator

## Filter Partners By→

Partner Role

Solution Provider?

**Solution Provider:** A trusted advisor that creates Cloudflare-based solutions that achieves measurable business outcomes for the customer.

MSSP?

**Managed Security Service Provider (MSSP):** A trusted partner that creates and manages Cloudflare-based services that achieves measurable business outcomes for the customer.

Distributor?

**Distributor:** Provides a wide-array of service and support to better enable Cloudflare partners to deliver business outcomes.

Partner Designations

Cloudflare One Design Partner Designated?

**Cloudflare One Design Partner Designated:** Is an elite designation for select partners that have made a significant investment in building a world-class Cloudflare One practice.

Partner Service Specializations

Application Security Authorized Service Delivery (ASD) Specialized?

**Application Security Authorized Service Delivery (ASD) Specialized:** An authorized advisor with the advanced capabilities to consistently deliver the highest quality Application Security assessment and deployment services.

Cloudflare One Authorized Service Delivery (ASD) Specialized?

**Cloudflare One Authorized Service Delivery (ASD) Specialized:** An authorized advisor with the advanced capabilities to consistently deliver the highest quality Cloudflare One assessment and deployment services.

Partner Product Specializations

Application Security Specialized?

**Application Security Specialized:** A specialized advisor with the fundamental and essential capabilities to design Application Security solutions that achieve measurable business outcomes.

Cloudflare One Specialized?

**Cloudflare One Specialized (formerly Zero Trust Specialized):** A specialized advisor with the fundamental and essential capabilities to design Cloudflare One solutions that achieve measurable business outcomes.
